豐興 (2015 TT)  Neutral

獲利成長有限，目前評價合理。  
投資建議 ：預估豐興 2026 年營收為 278.71 億元， YoY-4.01% ，
稅後 EPS 為 4.23 元，考量 (1) 豐興本業營運穩定，每年獲利維持
在 4 元左右。 (2) 今年中國對鋼鐵出口實施許可證制度後，中國
鋼材出口量先降後升，不利亞洲鋼市價格維持。 (3) 上游原材價
格持續偏弱，國際廢鋼價格近一季平均下跌 10% ，後續觀察鋼
價能否築底。綜上所述，對豐興的投資建議為 Neutral 。  
預估 3Q26 營收 70.17 億元， YoY+5.61% ， QoQ-7.52% ，稅後
EPS 0.99 元：2Q26 受惠於產業旺季來臨及亞洲鋼價回升，推動
豐興營運成長 ，2Q26 營收 75.87 億元 ，YoY-9.20% ，QoQ+18.47% ，
毛利率 9.67% ，稅後 EPS 1.46 元，符合預期。 3Q26 預期受夏季
電價高峰及鋼價下跌影響，營收將略為下降至 70.17 億元，
YoY+5.61% ， QoQ-7.52% ，稅後 EPS 0.99 元。  
上游原材價格持續偏弱，國際廢鋼價格近一季平均下跌 10% ，
後續觀察鋼價能否築底： 從原料價格來看，國際鐵礦砂目前價
格約為 96 美元 /噸，亞洲廢鋼價格約 365 美元 /噸，近一季下跌
約 10% ，主要受到需求端疲弱的影響而走弱，認為由於原料價
格再下跌的空間有限，觀察 4Q26 鋼價是否有機會逐漸築底。  
今年中國對鋼鐵出口實施許可證制度後，中國鋼材出口量先降
後升，不利亞洲鋼市價格維持： 中國宣佈 2026 年開始對鋼鐵實
施出口許可證制度 ， 政策實施後觀察中國的粗鋼產量在 1Q26 確
實有明顯下降的趨勢出現，但進入 2Q26 後鋼鐵產出已有復甦
回升的跡象出現，產量縮減的趨勢已經結束。顯示在鋼鐵價格
拉高後，大多鋼鐵廠眼見有利可圖因而增加生產供應。 06/2026
中國的鋼材出口量回升到 1,032 萬噸， YoY+6.6% 。在生產面，
08/2026 上旬中國粗鋼日產量約 267 萬噸，與去年同期持平。中
國鋼材出口量回升，不利亞洲鋼市價格維持。

### Page 2

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 9 月 1 日

2 群益投顧 
  豐興的 ESG ：  
豐興致力於從製程改善提升減碳成效，如將鋼筋工場改為直接軋延、將條線
與型鋼工場加熱爐燃料從重油改為天然氣等， 2021 年更成立淨零排碳推動
小組，從低碳生產、優化設備能耗跟導入再生能源等面向實踐淨零碳排，每
季召開會議討論進度並向董事會報告。在規劃淨零碳排路徑時，改善製程碳
排將合金鋼原料投入量降低 10%，逐年替換高效低耗設備以降低碳排，長期
將規劃自建與購買再生能源以降低電力碳排。  
預估 3Q26 營收 70.17 億元， YoY+5.61% ， QoQ-7.52% ，稅後 EPS 0.99 元：
2Q26 受惠於產業旺季來臨及亞洲鋼價回升，推動豐興營運成長， 2Q26 營收
75.87 億元， YoY-9.20% ， QoQ+18.47% ，毛利率 9.67% ，稅後 EPS 1.46 元，
符合預期。  
3Q26 預期受夏季電價高峰及鋼價下跌影響，營收將略為下降至 70.17 億元，
YoY+5.61% ， QoQ-7.52% ，稅後 EPS 0.99 元。  
上游原材價格持續偏弱，國際廢鋼價格近一季平均下跌 10% ，後續觀察鋼
價能否築底：  
從原料價格來看，國際鐵礦砂目前價格約為 96 美元 /噸，亞洲廢鋼價格約
365 美元 /噸，近一季下跌約 10%，主要受到需求端疲弱的影響而走弱，認為
由於原料價格再下跌的空間有限，觀察 4Q26 鋼價是否有機會逐漸築底。  
今年中國對鋼鐵出口實施許可證制度後，中國鋼材出口量先降後升，不利亞
洲鋼市價格維持：  
中國宣佈 2026 年開始對鋼鐵實施出口許可證制度，政策實施後觀察中國的
粗鋼產量在 1Q26 確實有明顯下降的趨勢出現，但進入 2Q26 後鋼鐵產出已
有復甦回升的跡象出現，產量縮減的趨勢已經結束。顯示在鋼鐵價格拉高
後，大多鋼鐵廠眼見有利可圖因而增加生產供應。 06/2026 中國的鋼材出口
量回升到 1,032 萬噸， YoY+6.6% 。在生產面， 08/2026 上旬中國粗鋼日產量
約 267 萬噸，與去年同期持平。中國鋼材出口量回升，不利亞洲鋼市價格維
持。  
投資建議：  
預估豐興 2026 年營收為 278.71 億元， YoY-4.01% ，稅後 EPS 為 4.23 元。  
考量 (1) 豐興本業營運穩定，每年獲利維持在 4 元左右。 (2) 今年中國對鋼鐵
出口實施許可證制度後，中國鋼材出口量先降後升，不利亞洲鋼市價格維
持。 (3) 上游原材價格持續偏弱，國際廢鋼價格近一季平均下跌 10% ，後續
觀察鋼價能否築底。綜上所述，對豐興的投資建議為 Neutral 。

.

6 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

### Page 1

電機機械  
上銀(2049.TT) 
AI 與半導體需求強勁，精密線性傳動元件交期持續拉長

AI 與半導體需求強勁，帶動工具機新一波的景氣循環，上銀生產
的精密線性傳動元件大量用於 AI 與半導體設備 ， 在半導體產業CapEx
確立的情況下，預期這波拉貨潮將延續至明年。在上一波 2021-22 年
的上升循環中，股價最高來到當年度 EPS 的 45 倍，考量到目前僅出
現第一波漲價，研究部以 2027 年 EPS 13.16 元給予 35x 本益比，目
標價為 460 元，給予買進的評等。

1. 半導體設備需求強勁，帶動工具機新一波的景氣循環 
上銀生產的線性滑軌與滾珠螺桿大量應用於 AI 與半導體設備當中，這
波景氣上升循環由從 4Q25 開始，拉貨動能逐季提升。從交期來看，線
性滑軌的交期從去年年底 3 個月，拉長到現在的 4~5 個月；滾珠螺桿
交期從去年年底 4 個月，拉長到現在 5~6 個月。從營收來看，扣除 2
月份有過年因素外 (去年過年在 1 月)，上銀的月營收 YoY 從 3 月的
17.4%一路提升到 8 月的 42.3%，此期間沒有縮小過，顯示營收不但在
成長，成長的動能還在加速。公司亦提到從 6 月開始 BB raio 大於 1，
需求非常強勁，已開始規劃明年過年期間要如何加班生產。研究部看好
這波由 AI 與半導體帶動的工具機需求延續至明年，預估上銀 2026/27
年營收為 300.1/378.2 億元，+23.7%/+26.0% YoY。

2. 市場供不應求，交期持續拉長看好後續漲價機會 
市場供不應求 ， 公司於今年3 月宣布調漲 7~15%， 已於6 月開始反應 。
2Q26 毛利率 34.2%，季增 2.2 個百分點，為稼動率與價格同步提升所
致，預估 3Q26 漲價效應完全反應後毛利率將進一步提升至 35.2%，研
究部預估上銀 2026/27 年的毛利率為 34.3%/35.6%， 分別年增5.5/1.3
個百分點，EPS 為 9.56/13.16 元，+121.7%/+37.6% YoY。 
這波由半導體產業 CapEx 所帶動的景氣上升，研究部看好在交期不斷拉長
的情況下，後續還有漲價、上修毛利率的機會。(上一波 2021-2022 年上升
循環中毛利率最高來到 37-38%)

一、 一、公司介紹 
   上銀科技成立於 1989 年 ， 專注於線性滑軌 、 滾珠螺桿與單軸機器人等精密線性
傳動元件的製造 ， 為該領域的全球領導廠商之一 。 除了持續研發線性傳動產品以外 ，
也自行開發出多種半導體自動化設備 ，產品廣泛應用於汽車 、 半導體 、 醫療 、 國防 、
航太及食品等產業。上銀憑藉自有品牌 「HIWIN」 的深厚研發實力與品質行銷全世
界，目前為全球第二大生產線性滑軌與滾珠螺桿的廠商，第一大為日商 THK 
(6481.T)。1H26 產品營收占比﹔線性滑軌 60%、 滾珠螺桿20%、 工業機器人12%、
其他 8%。2025 年地區營收占比﹔台灣 44%、中國 21%、德國 16%、美國 5%、
日本 4%、其他 10%。(地區營收 2025 年已是最新公布)

二、 產業分析 
上銀主要產品為線性滑軌與滾珠螺桿，產品功能如下﹔ 
1. 線性滑軌﹔一種線性傳動元件，由滑塊與滑軌組成，透過內部的滾珠或滾  
柱循環系統來降低摩擦，讓平台保持平行穩定的直線運動。  
2. 滾珠螺桿﹔一種線性傳動元件，由螺桿、螺帽與鋼珠組成，利用鋼珠在螺桿與
螺帽間循環滾動，將旋轉運動轉換為直線運動。

商業模式 
線性滑軌與滾珠螺桿為工具機零組件 ， 工具機產業商業模式分為以下三種 (以 AOI
檢測設備為例)﹔ 
1. 工具機零組件廠生產線性滑軌 ➨ 工具機 OEM 廠將各零組件組裝成五軸加工
機 ➨ 設備零組件廠用五軸加工機生產高精度定位平台  ➨ 設備商用高精度定位
平台與其他零組件組裝成 AOI 檢測設備。  
2. 工具機零組件廠生產線性滑軌 ➨ 工具機 OEM 廠將各零組件組裝成五軸加工
機 ➨ 設備商用五軸加工機自行生產所需的高精度定位平台或其他零組件。 
3. 工具機零組件廠生產的線性滑軌同時是工具機零組件與設備零組件  ➨ 設備
商直接購買線性滑軌來組裝。

圖五、商業模式

資料來源：統一投顧整理

### Page 5

請參見末頁重要披露及免責聲明 5

產業趨勢 
JMTBA (日本工具機協會) 每月會將日本所有工具機 OEM 廠的訂單加總 ， 公布 「日
本工具機訂單金額」 ，其產品包含多軸加工機、CNC 車床、滾齒機……等等，由於
日本擁有全球規模前幾大的工具機 OEM 廠，如 DMG、Yamazaki、Okuma，且多
年來產品遍布各個產業所累積的經驗也使得這些公司不論在自研控制器的演算
法，還是機器長時間運轉下維持的精度與溫度都與其他國家有技術上的差距。  
因此，在各行各業景氣轉好，需要大量的工具機時，JMTBA 公布的訂單金額被視
為是工具機產業最重要的觀察指標，工具機 OEM 廠訂單增加 ➨ 增加向工具機
零組件廠的拉貨。 
觀察過去十年日本工具機訂單的走勢，可發現景氣大約呈現兩年上升、兩年下降
的循環。上一次的上升週期為 2021-22 年疫情加上塞港，全球各產業瘋狂拉貨，
使得工具機訂單 YoY 從 2020/11 的-6%，最高拉升到 2021/5 的 142%，一直到
2022/10 訂單 YoY 才轉負 。 塞港造成的恐慌式補庫存為工具機產業帶來20 年來不
曾有過的成長率 ， 也使得隨後各產業去化庫存時 ， 工具機這次的景氣休息得較久 ，
直到 2H25 才再次迎來上升週期。

圖六、日本工具機訂單及 YoY

資料來源：JMTBA

圖七、上銀過去十年股價走勢圖

資料來源：Wind 
（註：與日本工具機訂單 YoY 高度聯動）

而在此波的上升週期，生產線性滑軌與滾珠螺桿等工具機零組件全球市占前二的
廠商，THK、上銀一致提到這一次的需求來自 AI 與半導體，且需求非常強勁。 
細看 AI 與半導體產業，其中涵蓋了相當多工具機的蹤跡，舉凡伺服器散熱所需的
水冷板、快接頭及熱交換器，到伺服器機殼、Compute/GPU Tray 與各種結構件，
都需要以工具機進行切削或沖壓。半導體產業中，在製程升級與產能擴充的過程
需要大量的半導體設備 ，不論在前 、 中 、 後段的設備都須使用線性傳動元件 ， 如蝕
刻機、AOI 檢測設備、各類型 Bonder 等等。 
產品規格方面，AI 與半導體所需的工具機精度更高，使得 THK、上銀這種技術領
先的廠商進一步受惠 。 一般工業用工具機僅需使用H/N 級的線性滑軌與 C5/C7 級
的滾珠螺桿，生產水冷板與快接頭時需使用 SP/P 級的線性滑軌與 C3 級的滾珠螺
桿；在Die Bonder (晶粒鍵合機)與 Flip Chip Bonder (倒裝鍵合機)上則需使用 UP/SP
級的線性滑軌與 C0/C1 級的滾珠螺桿，生產高精度的線性滑軌與滾珠螺桿毛利率
顯著高於精度較低的。

表二、線性滑軌規格

SP ≈ 7 μm 
P ≈ 15 μm

除了做元件以外，上銀也與大銀微系統合作製造半導體整機設備，由上銀做線性
滑軌與滾珠螺桿 ，大銀微提供各類馬達 、 定位平台 、 驅動器與運動控制器等等 ，產
品包含 EFEM ( 晶圓移載系統 )、Load Port ( 晶圓裝卸機)、Wafer Robot ( 晶圓機器
人) 、Wafer Aligner (晶圓尋邊器)，目前認列於機器人營收當中，產能滿載，直接
出貨給半導體製程廠商。

小結 
歷經 2022 年後的全球各產業庫存去化，工具機景氣委靡了接近三年的期間，如
今挾帶著 AI 伺服器與半導體設備需求強勁成長，再次迎來新一波景氣上升循環。
從水冷板、快接頭到各類半導體設備，皆帶動工具機需求快速升溫， 相關廠商也
同步指出需求強勁、訂單持續增加，交期明顯拉長。 
更重要的是，這波需求主要來自半導體設備等高精度應用領域，所需線性滑軌與
滾珠螺桿規格高於一般工業產品，上銀除受惠於工具機景氣重新向上外，亦可進
一步受惠於高規格精密線性傳動元件需求成長。

三、 三、營運狀況 
1. 1. 半導體設備需求強勁，帶動工具機新一波的景氣循環 
上銀生產的線性滑軌與滾珠螺桿大量應用於半導體設備當中，這波景氣上升循
環由從 4Q25 開始，拉貨動能逐季提升。從交期來看，線性滑軌的交期從去年
年底 3 個月 ，到五月法說會提到 3.5 個月 ， 再到八月份交期已經拉長到 5 個月 ；
滾珠螺桿交期從去年年底 4 個月，到五月法說會的 4.5 個月，再到現在的 6 個
月。從營收來看，扣除 2 月份有過年因素外 (去年過年在 1 月)，上銀的月營收
YoY 從 3 月的 17.4%一路提升到 8 月的 42.3%，此期間沒有縮小過，顯示營收
不但在成長，成長的動能還在加速。公司亦提到從6 月開始 BB raio 大於 1，需
求非常強勁，已開始規劃明 年過年期間要如何加班生產。研究部看好這波半導
體帶動的工具機需求延續至明年 ，預估上銀 2026/27 年營收為 300.1/378.2 億
元，+23.7%/+26.0% YoY。

圖十二、上銀各產品交期

資料來源：統一投顧整理

2. 2. 市場供不應求，交期持續拉長看好後續漲價機會 
市場供不應求，公司於今年 3 月宣布調漲 7~15%，已於 6 月開始反應。2Q26 毛
利率 34.2%，季增 2.2 個百分點，為稼動率與價格同步提升所致，預估 3Q26 漲價
效應完全反應後毛利率將進一步提升至 35.2%，研究部預估上銀 2026/27 年的毛
利率為 34.3%/35.6%，分別年增 5.5/1.3 個百分點 ，EPS 為 9.56/13.16 元，
+121.7%/+37.6% YoY。這波由半導體產業 CapEx 所帶動的景氣上升，研究部看
好在交期不斷拉長的情況下，後續還有漲價、上修毛利率的機會。 (上一波 2021-
2022 年上升循環中毛利率由 31%迅速拉升至 37-38%)

3. 預期景氣將延續至明年，目前評價偏低 
AI 與半導體需求強勁，帶動工具機新一波的景氣循環，上銀生產的精密線性傳動
元件大量用於 AI 與半導體設備，在半導體產業 CapEx 確立的情況下，預期這波
拉貨潮將延續至明年。在上一波 2021-22 年的上升循環中，股價最高來到當年度
EPS 的 45 倍，考量到目前僅出現第一波漲價，研究部以 2027 年 EPS 13.16 元給
予 35x 本益比，目標價為 460 元，給予買進的評等。

附錄 一 ：評等之標準  
評等  定義  
強力買進     預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬超過 25 %以上  
買進  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬 10% 以上  
中立  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 10～ -10%  
降低持股  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 (-10% )以下

全新 (2455 TT)  Buy

2H26 、 2027 年營收動能可期，評等為 Buy。  
投資建議 ：07/2026 全新營收 4.22 億元， MoM+29.48% ，創歷史
新高，主要係 AI data center 相關需求持續熱絡，且出貨表現亦
隨磷化銦基板料況改善而回歸常軌所致，且 2H26、2027 年營收
動能仍可期，故群益對全新的投資建議為 Buy 。  
07/2026 營收創新高： 2Q26 營收 9.65 億元， QoQ+0.63% ，其中，
微電子營收 7.04 億元， QoQ-1.89% ，與 1Q26 相去有限；光電子
產品營收 2.61 億元， QoQ+8.12% ，但 01~06/2026 僅 02/2026 受
農曆春節影響而僅 0.68 億元，其餘各月份光電子營收均為
0.86~0.88 億元，換言之，缺料問題仍影響光電子產品出貨表現。  
不過， 07/2026 營收 4.22 億元， MoM+29.48% ，創歷史新高，關
鍵在於磷化銦基板缺料狀況將隨中國政府 05/2026 、 07/2026 兩
度放行管制而改善 ， 帶動全新光電子產品營收增加至 1.69 億元 ，
MoM+93.74% ；至於微電子產品營收 2.53 億元，亦為近幾年相
對高水準。  
2H26 展望優於 1H26：雖然 AI data center 相關需求熱絡，然而，
01~06/2026 光電子產品月營收成長性仍受制於磷化銦基板缺
料。全新磷化銦基板過去多數由美國業者的中國廠供應，然而，
受到中國政府管制磷化銦基板出口數量影響， 4Q25 全新光電子
產品營收逐月減少，且全新來自該供應商的磷化銦基板庫存
12/2025 已陸續用罄， 01~06/2026 磷化銦基板主要由第 2、 3 供
應商供應。  
所幸，中國政府 05/2026 月底批准 2026 年第 1 批磷化銦基板出
口， 07/2026 則再通過第 2 批，且總數量大於第 1 批，第 1、 2
批合計數量更已超過 2025 年總數量，此外，全新 06/2026 已陸
續接獲第 1 批獲准出口的磷化銦基板並用以生產光電子產品，
2H26 展望優於 1H26 。

2 群益投顧 
  企業 ESG 資訊揭露：  
就企業溫室氣體排放量減量目標而言，由於全新專注於製造、銷售及研發化
合物半導體磊晶片，溫室氣體排放量多寡，主要和能源耗用、產能及產品組
成有關。全新乃就單位產品的能源耗用與溫室氣體排放量，設定目標為每年
減少 5%，並將視產能擴充與產品變化情形，賡續追蹤與調整設定與目標。
至於企業產品或服務帶給客戶或消費者之減碳效果的部份，全新為客戶代
工生產的產品多數運用於通訊等電子產品，非屬終端消費性產品；全新除協
助客戶設計低耗能、高效率產品，以降低產品生命週期內的資源耗用外，亦
持續進行生產製造過程的節能與減廢，以降低對環境所造成的衝擊，減少單
位產品所消耗的資源與污染物，為客戶及社會創造減碳環保效益。

6 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

Update
MediaTek  | Asia Pacific
NVIDIA and Alphabet Invest in
MediaTek’s CB for Extensive
Partnership in ASIC ; Stay OW,
Top Pick
Our thoughts
• In the Bloomberg interview (link), NVIDIA (covered by Joe Moore) and
MediaTek CEOs together answered questions about the rationale of the
partnership and the strategic investment. Essentially, through the NVLink
Fusion platform, MediaTek’s XPU customers can use NVIDIA’s scale-up and
scale-out networking technology to build an AI server system.

• Although MediaTek did not change its 2027 ASIC TAM and market share
guidance given this extensive partnership and said it would take time, we do
think it will increase the chance for MediaTek to win a second CSP customer
(see our report MediaTek: Smartphone weak, but Cloud AI taking off).
MediaTek during the interview on August 31, indicated it is indeed engaging
with some customers.
• MediaTek indicated the proceeds of the CB will be use for the chip supply

chain, which we believe should include some key components (e.g., HBM and
ABF substrate) procurement and wafer foundry capacity prepayment.
• All in all, we see upside to our 2028 ASIC revenue assumption, given: 1)
higher chance to win a second or even third CSP customers; 2) higher
revenue from material preparation; 3) solidified partnership with Alphabet in
TPU (see our report AI Supply Chain: Google ASIC, who gets what for design
services?). Our PT of NT$5,588 implies 42% upside.

Henry Zhao
Equity Analyst

Valuation Methodology and Risks
MediaTek (2454.TW)
Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions: cost of equity 9.2%, intermediate growth
rate 12.0%, terminal growth rate 3.0%.
Risks to Upside
n Edge AI proliferates smartphone replacement cycle.
n Smartphone demand rises in China and other EMs.
n New products attract high demand, resulting in market share gains.
n Google's TPU spurs demand.
Risks to Downside

n Smartphone demand deteriorates in China and other EMs.
n Competition heats up, resulting in pricing competition.
n New products attract low demand, causing market share loss.
n Margin dilution is more severe.

leaving headroom unused, and no target or ceiling has been set for Nvidia’s eventual

Partnership expanded across AI infrastructure, local AI computing and n
automotive: Alongside the pricing, Nvidia and MediaTek announced a deepening of
their longstanding collaboration spanning three areas, including 1) AI
infrastructure: MediaTek will adopt Nvidia’s NVLink Fusion platform to enable
customers to develop custom AI infrastructure designed to integrate with Nvidia

rack-scale systems and AI factories. 2) Local AI computing: the companies will
continue to collaborate on multiple generations of RTX Spark™ and DGX Spark™ PC
chips integrating Nvidia GPUs with MediaTek SoCs. 3) Automotive: MediaTek and
Nvidia will continue developing platforms for AI-powered, software-defined vehicles
in the era of physical AI.
Key highlights from the joint interview
Following the announcement, the CEOs of Nvidia and MediaTek were interviewed jointly

by Bloomberg TV. Below we summarize key highlights from the interview.
Nvidia characterized the partnership with MediaTek as a multi-generation and n
long-term collaboration. Nvidia commented that the two companies are working
together across multiple generations of edge AI and PC SoCs, while the expanded
partnership will also cover data-center XPUs, networking and robotic systems.
Nvidia added that it is a decade-long roadmap.
The expanded collaboration will allow MediaTek’s XPUs to connect directly into n

Nvidia’s broader AI factory architecture. Nvidia noted that it will connect its
NVLink Fusion scale-up system, Spectrum-X switches and broader networking
infrastructure to MediaTek’s XPUs.  According to Nvidia, this would allow MediaTek
XPUs to be integrated into data centers already running Nvidia infrastructure, while
MediaTek XPU project wins could also create opportunities for Nvidia networking
products.
MediaTek expects the partnership to support growth in its AI ASIC business, n

although the new revenue contribution will take time. When asked whether the
collaboration could accelerate MediaTek’s AI chip business, MediaTek said that it
expects this to happen, but noted that it would take some time as the company is
only beginning to integrate Nvidia’s latest NVLink version into the relevant chassis.
MediaTek highlighted faster time-to-market for customers as a key benefit.
Both companies indicated that customer engagement around NVLink Fusion has n

already started. Nvidia said the collaboration was in large part driven by customer
requests and that there are already customers committed to NVLink Fusion.
Nvidia positioned NVLink Fusion as a way to accommodate custom XPUs within n
its existing infrastructure rather than as a replacement for its broader platform.
Nvidia noted that XPUs are already present in the market and said it is opening its
platform to allow customers’ specialized XPUs to connect more easily to Nvidia

infrastructure. Nvidia added that it expects to continue participating through
networking, switching and CPUs, even in AI factories where some racks incorporate
XPUs.

Goldman Sachs MediaTek (2454.TW)For the exclusive use of KEVINLU@LENOVO.COM

### Page 3

Nvidia indicated that the partnership enables MediaTek to offer a more n
complete end-to-end XPU solution. Nvidia noted that an XPU provider would
otherwise need to address multiple system-level components, including NVLink
switching, NICs, Ethernet switching and scale-up/scale-out networking. Through the
partnership, these components can be provided through Nvidia’s existing
infrastructure.
MediaTek plans to use the bond proceeds across supply-chain and technology n

investments. MediaTek said supply-chain investment will be one use of proceeds
and emphasized that the company’s supply chain is global. The company also plans
to invest further in design capabilities, ASIC chip capabilities, IP and interconnects,
and advanced packaging, with the proceeds supporting the expanded Nvidia
collaboration.
The collaboration also extends to Nvidia’s custom HBM architecture. Nvidia n
indicated that its next-generation custom HBM will use an Nvidia-designed base

layer that has been extended to NVLink Fusion partners, including MediaTek and
Amazon. Nvidia noted that the NVHBM and XPU die will be integrated into a CoWoS
package, allowing partners to leverage Nvidia’s installed base and supply-chain
scale.
MediaTek identified the supply chain as its primary bottleneck. MediaTek said n
supply-chain challenges extend beyond HBM to substrates and other components,
while noting that its location in Taiwan and close collaboration with Nvidia are

helpful. Nvidia added that its networking ecosystem is becoming part of MediaTek’s
supply chain, while MediaTek’s XPU is becoming part of Nvidia’s supply chain.
Nvidia sees a solid standalone business case in its MediaTek investment. In n
response to questions on financing, Nvidia noted that MediaTek operates its own
independent business and is already a highly profitable and successful company.
Nvidia indicated the investment is grounded in the companies’ deep, multi-year

technology partnership, with the current roadmap extending over a decade, and is
intended to demonstrate Nvidia’s confidence in and commitment to the
collaboration. Nvidia also expects the investment to generate attractive returns.
Our thoughts - Strong ecosystem endorsement, broader system-level
opportunity and potential new TAM into custom HBM base die
Strong endorsement from two leading AI ecosystem partners
We believe the most immediate takeaway is the strong endorsement from both Nvidia

and Alphabet. Nvidia subscribed to US$3.5bn of MediaTek’s US$3.9bn CB issuance,
while Alphabet and global institutional investors participated in the remaining
US$400mn. In our view, Nvidia’s capital commitment provides strong validation of
MediaTek’s silicon, connectivity and advanced packaging capabilities. Alphabet’s
participation also reinforces MediaTek’s relevance across multiple major AI ecosystems.
Funding should strengthen supply-chain security and support continued technology

investment. According to the company, one of the original purposes of the financing
was to secure capacity and supply for constrained components such as HBM.
Although certain customers currently procure HBM and other components directly, we
1 September 2026   3
Goldman Sachs MediaTek (2454.TW)For the exclusive use of KEVINLU@LENOVO.COM
e92c7a75ab8b4efbba794e6b187208c8

### Page 4

believe MediaTek could assume greater supply-chain responsibility under future
engagement models, including entering into capacity or supply agreements on behalf of
customer projects.
The company also indicated that it plans to invest further in its technology and design
capabilities, including ASIC design, IP , interconnects and advanced packaging, etc. We
believe the additional funding should provide MediaTek with greater flexibility to
secure the resources required for increasingly complex AI related programs.

The partnership broadens MediaTek’s role from custom AI silicon toward system-level
enablement
Based on the interview and the company announcement, we believe MediaTek is seeking
to extend its role beyond designing and delivering an individual AI ASIC toward helping
customers address a broader portion of the system surrounding the XPU. This could
include connectivity, memory architecture, advanced packaging, manufacturing and
integration with rack-scale AI infrastructure.

However, we do not believe this means MediaTek intends to build or sell complete
AI racks. Rather, MediaTek can help customers integrate their custom XPUs into
Nvidia’s pre-validated chassis, networking and rack-scale architectures, allowing
customers to deploy and scale their AI infrastructure more efficiently.
NVLink Fusion is intended to provide customers with a faster path from custom XPU to
AI factory. Customers can focus their resources on the differentiated compute portion of

the accelerator while relying on Nvidia and MediaTek for surrounding technologies such
as NVLink connectivity, high-speed SerDes, memory architecture, packaging,
manufacturing and system qualification.
Instead of independently engineering and qualifying every component surrounding the
custom XPU, customers can use NVIDIA ’s prebuilt, prequalified and system-prevalidated
platform as a starting point. We believe this should reduce integration complexity and

risk, shorten time to market and make it easier for customers to deploy custom XPUs
within existing Nvidia-based data centers and scale them into rack-level AI factories.
Incremental revenue will likely take time, but the partnership expands MediaTek’s
longer-term opportunity
MediaTek and Nvidia did not provide a timeline for the first NVLink Fusion-related
customer, product or revenue contribution. The company indicated that it is only
beginning to integrate the latest version of NVLink, after which customers would still

need to complete their own product development, qualification and deployment
processes. We therefore see limited immediate earnings implications and believe the
incremental opportunity is more likely to emerge over the medium to long term.
Nevertheless, the partnership broadens MediaTek’s addressable opportunity from
designing an individual AI ASIC toward enabling a production-ready system. We
believe this could improve customer win rates and increase MediaTek’s potential content

and value capture per project through additional contributions from connectivity,
memory architecture, advanced packaging, manufacturing and system integration.
1 September 2026   4
Goldman Sachs MediaTek (2454.TW)For the exclusive use of KEVINLU@LENOVO.COM
e92c7a75ab8b4efbba794e6b187208c8

### Page 5

The relationship extends across the full edge-to-cloud AI computing stack 
Beyond AI infrastructure, the partnership covers multiple generations of DGX Spark and 
RTX Spark chips for local AI computing, as well as automotive platforms. We therefore 
view the announcement as a broader, multi-generation technology alignment spanning 
cloud AI factories, enterprise and local AI computing, PCs and physical AI, rather than a 
single-product collaboration. 
Management noted that the Nvidia partnership should not restrict MediaTek’s existing 
custom ASIC engagements. We see NVLink Fusion as an additional architecture option 
rather than an exclusive platform. Customers will be able to decide whether to adopt the 
Nvidia ecosystem depending on their infrastructure and workload requirements, while 
MediaTek can continue supporting customers using other architectures. 
In our view, Alphabet’s participation in the CB issuance should allay any concerns 
some investors may have had that the closer Nvidia relationship could affect 
MediaTek’s existing hyperscaler engagements. Instead, we believe MediaTek’s 
positioning is to remain architecture-flexible while providing customers with a broader 
range of design and system-integration options. 
NVHBM collaboration extends MediaTek’s participation into the memory and 
packaging architecture 
We note that Nvidia’s next-generation NVHBM will use a custom HBM base layer 
designed by Nvidia, which has also been extended to NVLink Fusion partners including 
MediaTek and Amazon. Nvidia noted that NVHBM will be integrated with the XPU die in 
a CoWoS package, allowing partners to leverage NVIDIA ’s broader HBM and packaging 
supply chain. While the NVHBM base layer itself is NVIDIA-designed, we believe 
MediaTek’s participation could deepen its involvement in memory architecture, 
interconnect and advanced packaging integration for XPU programs. Separately, 
MediaTek has highlighted custom HBM and memory-interface capabilities as part 
of its broader XPU offering, which we believe could provide incremental content 
opportunities for customer-specific designs outside Nvidia’s standardized NVHBM 
implementation. 
Investment Thesis, Price Target Risks and Methodology

Investment Thesis - MediaTek 
MediaTek is a leading global IC design house specializing in smartphone AP (application 
processor). We have a positive stance on MediaTek, viewing it as well-positioned to 
transition from a traditional smartphone application provider to an AI-focused vendor, 
beginning with AI smartphones and extending to enterprise ASICs and smart automotive 
solutions (in partnership with NVIDIA) in 2025 and beyond. We expect MediaTek to 
achieve solid multi-year growth, where we expect revenue and earnings to increase by 
43%/59% CAGRs, respectively, in 2025-28E. This growth will be primarily driven by: 1) 
market share gains, particularly in the premium segment (specifically high-end 5G 
flagship SoCs), 2) strong ramp in AI ASIC business, and 3) new TAM in the 
automotive/computing sectors.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

Greater China Technology Hardware

SEMICON Taiwan 2026
Takeaways
Key takeaways from CEO fireside chat, which included Hon Hai (HH) Chairman
Young Liu and Unimicron (UMTC) Chairman Shan-Chieh Chien:
• HH sees AI demand as  unprecedented in speed and scale, pressuring the
supply chain to expand capacity while raising capital intensity.
• Management views diversification as structural, accelerated by geopolitics
and countries' push for local production. We have written about this here,

here, and here.
• HH's strategy is "Made with Taiwan", exporting Taiwan's manufacturing IP,
system-integration know-how and execution speed to end markets via local
partnerships and production.
• UMTC said AI lifts ABF substrate demand and complexity (large sizes, higher
layer counts, more advanced designs), widening supply constraints. This
aligns with our view, which we have written here, here, and here.
• UMTC said it has held more than 10 meetings in the past 18 months with

suppliers and customers to align demand forecast and capacity, by actively
preparing capacity, preparing new facilities, securing land.
• Supply of upstream key materials, such as ABR and T-glass, remains tight.

companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of
interest that could affect the objectivity of Morgan Stanley
Research. Investors should consider Morgan Stanley

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.

below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Elite Material
Valuation
Our target price for EMC is set at NT$6,000, based on a target PE multiple of 30x on our 2027E EPS average. We believe our
target PE multiple, at the peak of its forward PE average in the past 5 years, is justified by a strong earnings outlook supported
by its leading position in CCL supply for AI GPU and ASIC (high m/s) from 2026E onwards. We also believe the continued spec

upgrade for CCL and price hikes should help support its margin expansion going forward. At our target price, the shares would
trade at 23.6x/13.2x 2026E/27E PB.
Risks
Citi’s quant system rates EMC high-risk given high share price volatility, which we largely attribute to frequent debates over the
spec upgrade for next-gen AI servers. In general, we believe some investors initially tend to have high hope for aggressive spec
upgrade without sufficiently considering the scalability and cost structure of CCL products, which then leads to a shortfall in

the end. However, from a longer-term perspective, we see a decent and clear spec upgrade trend with meaningful ASP growth
potential among AI servers of different generations. As such, we do not assign a high-risk rating.
Key downside risks that could prevent the shares from reaching our target price include: 1) a slower-than-expected CCL
upgrade trend; 2) weaker-than-expected AI server demand; 3) production bottlenecks in AI supply chain (eg, glass, copper foil,

foundry or OSAT); 4) unexpected share loss in key AI server projects; and 5) stricter practice of OOC policy by end customers.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

Update
Accton Technology Corporation  | Asia Pacific
August Sales +4% MoM/+59%
YoY
In this report, we focus on Accton's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for share price movement.
Details:
• August sales were NT$40,873mn (+4% MoM/+59% YoY).
• QTD reached 66% of our 3Q26 estimate, at NT$120,883mn (+27% QoQ/
+66% YoY), and 70% of consensus, at NT$114,321mn (+20% QoQ/+57%
YoY).
Our view:
• We see 3Q26 revenue tracking in line with our estimates

• Our checks indicate that Accton has been negotiating with customers to let
them consign  those expensive components, which could weigh on its
revenue growth but should be positive to margins.
• Looking ahead, we expect the momentum to remain elevated into 2H26, as it
should benefit from broader-based migration to 800G network switches
from both existing and new customers, and the ramp of the new AI
accelerator model from its major customer should be in full swing.

• Stay OW with a PT of NT$3,500, based on 30x 2027 P/E.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Accton Technology Corporation (2345.TW)
Base case, P/E, which we believe better captures the company's earnings growth momentum.
We assign a target P/E of 30x to our 2027 EPS estimate, which we view as justified by an
operating profit CAGR of 48% for 2025-28, based on our estimates. This implies a PEG of
around 0.6, vs. its average of 0.7 over 2019-25, when the stock was trading at a P/E of 22x for

an operating profit CAGR of 32%.

n Stronger ODM direct business model adoption
Risks to Downside

ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Greater China Semiconductors  | Asia Pacific
Quantum Security / eMemory:
Investor Feedback
Low investor expectations, mostly a teach-in – eMemory
remains under-owned and under-researched, so feedback was
largely educational rather than push-back; we see this as the
marketing opportunity.
Morgan Stanley Taiwan Limited+
Daniel Yen, CFA
Equity Analyst
Daniel.Yen@morganstanley.com                                                +886 2 2730-2863

Greater China Technology Semiconductors
Asia Pacific
Industry View Attractive
What we published – and the feedback. On 17 August we released the Global
Foundation “Hardware Quantum Security – Building Defenses for Q-Day and
Beyond” with our eMemory OW initiation (PT NT$3,330): as quantum computing
nears (base case ~2030), a new hardware-security layer must be deployed 3-5 years
ahead, with physical unclonable function (PUF)-based Root-of-Trust IP adding 5-10%

to the semi IP market. Feedback was constructive – clients agree the “Harvest Now,
Decrypt Later” risk and 2030-31 migration deadlines make this a multi-year, non-
binary theme. The recurring point –  and our marketing opportunity – is that  Aspeed
is already well-owned while eMemory stays under-researched by global funds.
Debate 1 – sizing the TAM. The key question is whether PUF is truly required once
post-quantum cryptography (PQC) arrives. We size a PUF-PQC TAM of ~US$310mn

by 2030 (base), US$600mn bull / US$190mn bear, on ~25% PQC adoption and
~50% PUF attach, with datacenter/AI servers the largest royalty pool. Once PQC is
implemented, secure key generation/storage makes PUF the logical anchor – so
sensitivity sits on PQC pace, not PUF attach. We view eMemory’s NeoPUF as best-in-
class: near-zero bit-error rate, lower area/power, and superior advanced-node scaling
vs. SRAM PUF.
Debate 2 – is eMemory the right proxy? Yes. The case rests on the core royalty

engine: royalties are the majority of earnings and tied to customer production, so as
mix shifts to leading-edge nodes, higher wafer values lift royalty per design-win.
Advanced-node projects are already >50% of licensing  non-recurring engineering
(NRE) revenue. This is corroborated by the just-reported August sales of NT
$357mn, +42.8% Y/Y (8M26 +21.9% Y/Y, 8th straight month of growth);
royalties were 60.8% of 2Q26 revenue (+7.8% Y/Y) and management guides for

royalty growth to accelerate into 2H26 on advanced-node ASPs and new apps (OTP,
NeoFlash/1T Flash, PUF-PQC). (Continued in the next section)
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of
interest that could affect the objectivity of Morgan Stanley
Research. Investors should consider Morgan Stanley
Research as only a single factor in making their investment

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
Under-owned, mostly a teach-in: feedback was largely educational, not push-back
– the marketing opportunity; clients called the level “a good entry point”.
August sales momentum: NT$357mn, +42.8% Y/Y; 8M26 +21.9% Y/Y (8th
straight month of Y/Y growth).
Reiterate Overweight, PT NT$3,330: ~30% further upside, plus free optionality

on a US$310mn base-case PUF-PQC TAM by 2030.
September 8, 2026 09:00 PM GMT

### Page 2

M
 Idea
2

Our view and stock calls. Reiterate OW, PT NT$3,330 (~30% upside from NT$2,570) – a 
rare mix of a defensive, high-visibility royalty stream (100% gross margin, ~63% ROE) plus 
free optionality on the post-quantum build-out, alongside Synopsys (OW) and adopter 
Aspeed (OW). Catalysts: monthly-sales momentum, the advanced-node royalty ramp, PQC 
design-win news-flow and San Jose OCP in Oct 2026. Risks: 1) semi downturn, 2) PUF-PQC 
competition, 3) slower quantum/PQC adoption. Net-net, eMemory is an under-owned 
name where the royalty re-rating and quantum-security theme are both intact.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
eMemory Technology Inc (3529.TWO)
We derive our price target from a residual income model. We assume an 8.6% cost of equity
(2.0% risk-free rate, 6.0% equity risk premium, and 1.1 beta), a payout ratio of 90%, a medium-
term earnings growth rate of 23.0%, and a terminal growth rate of 4.4%. Based on these
assumptions, our RI model implies an equity value of NT$249bn, or NT$3,330 per share.

Risks to Upside
n Faster-than-expected PUF adoption
n Stronger-than-expected advanced-node OTP migration
n Higher-than-expected royalty expansion from wafer value growth
Risks to Downside
n Slower-than-expected adoption by key customers
n Slower-than-expected PQC adoption
n Increasing geopolitical and trade policy risks
Aspeed Technology (5274.TWO)
Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions:
n Cost of equity of 9.8% (2.0% risk-free rate, 6% risk premium, 1.3 beta)

n Medium-term growth rate of 21.4%
n Terminal growth rate of 5.5%
n Cash payout ratio of 85%
Risks to Upside
n Stronger cloud demand
n Faster-than-expected spec migration
n Mild competition
Risks to Downside
n Softening cloud demand
n Slower-than-expected spec migration
n Intensified competition
n Further policy tightening in China
Synopsys Inc. (SNPS.O)
The sector is trading on a 30-35x P/E multiple range. We expect Synopsys to trade in line and

apply a c.33x P/E multiple to estimated FY26 EPS ($15.12).
Risks to Upside
n Bigger ramp-up in AI ASICs and GPUs than modelled
n Quicker recovery in Automotive & industrial
n Design wins at expense of peers with Ansys products

n Strong recovery in IP business (esp. in China)
Risks to Downside
n Scale and duration of downturn seeing deeper R&D cuts
n Further tightening of export controls
n Loss of share to new entrant EDA players given localisation trends
n Failure to hit cost synergies from Ansys merger

12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

Document 106: 260909_daiwa_ardentec-buy-initiation

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

Investment case: We initiate coverage of Ardentec, a Taiwanese pure 
testing provider serving foundries, IDMs and fabless chipmakers, with a 
Buy (1) rating. We believe Ardentec is transitioning from an auto/industrial 
test vendor into an AI/HPC wafer-sort powerhouse, supported by a 35% 
revenue CAGR (2025-28E) and gross margin expansion to 45% by 2028E.

Longtan fab ramp with premium hourly rate. We expect the 7-story 
Longtan mega-fab, which is developing at one floor per quarter to deliver 
significant revenue contribution (c.TWD450m+/quarter per floor). We 
believe this underpins a structural upgrade in revenue, as advanced wafer 
sorting for application-specific integrated circuits (ASIC) commands an 
hourly billing rate nearly double that of legacy testing. Furthermore, we 
believe strategic collaborations with key Taiwan foundry leaders position 
Ardentec to capture expanding AI/HPC test allocations moving forward.

Gross margin increase driven by high utilisation rate. As its key peers 
prioritise merchant AI GPUs, displaced mobile/RF/networking orders are 
refilling Ardentec’s fully depreciated tester lines, lifting mature utilisation. 
Test-time inflation (2-3x on complex ASICs) anchors Longtan capacity at a 
90%+ utilisation rate. As fixed costs comprise 40-45% of COGS, this drives 
blended gross margin from 36% in 2025 to 45% in 2028E, with steady 
operating expense (9-10% of sales) flowing into operating profit.

Turnkey E/O testing for CPO and non-dilutive expansion. Leveraging 
synergies with Giga-Solution, Raytek (7873 TT; 25% stake), and strategic 
partner Fabrinet (FN US, NR), Ardentec builds a comprehensive electro-
optical test flow aligned with TSMC’s compact universal photonic engine 
(COUPE) platform. While co-packaged optics (CPO) testing accounts for 
<5% of near-term sales, it secures an early-mover moat in optical I/O.

Catalysts: 1) Completion of 7-floor capacity with premium rates and higher 
UTR; 2) peer spillover orders refilling fully depreciated capacity, expanding 
gross margin; 3) higher-than-expected ASIC and CPO contribution.

Valuation: Driven by a structural earnings inflection fuelled by Longtan fab 
expansion, expanding AI ASIC test pricing premiums, and high-margin peer 
spillover orders, we initiate Ardentec with a Buy (1) rating and 12-month TP 
of TWD340, based on a target PER of 25x applied to our 4-quarter-forward 
EPS, at the top end of its historical 10-25x range but supported by a 48% 
2025-28E EPS CAGR and peer AI test valuations of 18-24x.

Risks: 1) Delays in capacity expansion; 2) pricing pressure as a second 
supplier; and 3) slow automotive and industrial recovery.

9 September 2026

Ardentec

➢ Pure testing captures ASIC growth and order spillover from peers

### Page 3

Ardentec (3264 TT): 9 September 2026

How do we justify our view? 
Growth outlook Valuation Earnings revisions

Growth outlook

Ardentec: revenue and gross margin outlook   
We forecast Ardentec to enter a structural multi-year 
revenue growth cycle, rising from TWD14.0bn in 2025 to 
TWD34.4bn by 2028E, implying a 34.8% CAGR. This 
revenue growth is supported by the phased ramp-up of its 
Longtan fab facility, increasing exposure to premium AI 
ASIC and HPC wafer testing, and broad-based ASP 
expansion driven by tighter mature-node test capacity. 
Meanwhile, we expect improving utilisation coupled with 
growing contributions from fully depreciated legacy testers, 
to drive gross margin expansion from 36.3% in 2025 to 
45.0% by 2028E.

Source: Company, Daiwa forecasts

Valuation   Ardentec: 1-year-forward PER bands    
Given the current structural mix transition from cyclical 
automotives towards high-margin AI/HPC custom ASICs, 
amplified gross margin and operating leverage from the 
Longtan ramp, and ROE inflection likely to surpass its 2022 
historical peak of c.23% over 2026-28E, we initiate 
coverage of Ardentec with a Buy (1) rating and 12-month 
TP of TWD340, based on a target PER of 25.0x applied to 
our 4-quarter-forward EPS, at the top end of its historical 
10-25x range but supported by a 48% 2025-28E EPS 
CAGR and peer AI test valuations of 18-24x.

Source: Bloomberg

Earnings revisions

Ardentec: Bloomberg-consensus earnings revisions    
Bloomberg consensus raised its 2026-27E EPS estimates 
by 30-45%, driven by the rapid Longtan Floor 1 ramp, 
premium ASIC hourly rates, and high-margin peer spillover 
orders. The upward revisions likely reflect the completion 
of the remaining floors, test-time inflation driving higher 
testing hours, and internal capacity tightness unlocking 
pricing power across mature lines. Ardentec is currently 
covered by 4 brokers. We believe the market is overlooking 
this stock despite its structural fundamental turnaround 
outlook and depressed valuation.

Source: Bloomberg

Company profile 
Kinsus was founded in 2000 as a subsidiary of Asustek. The company mainly focus on niche ball grid 
array (BGA) substrates to avoid price competition among mainstream products like graphics card and 
chipsets, and it has become top-3 flip chip manufacturer in global. Besides, its manufacturing sites are 
based in Taiwan and China.

### Page 6

Ardentec (3264 TT): 9 September 2026 
From Automotive to AI/HPC growth engines 
A premier pure play in wafer testing 
Founded in October 1999 and headquartered in Hukou, Hsinchu, Ardentec Corporation is 
a premier semiconductor testing pure play, ranking among the top three pure test service 
providers in Taiwan in terms of revenue. While semiconductor testing is inherently a 
capital- and technology-intensive arena dominated by foundry and integrated OSAT titans 
such as TSMC (2330 TT, TWD2,465, Buy [1]; covered by Daiwa analyst Rick Hsu) and 
ASE Group (3711 TT, TWD640, Outperform [2]; covered by Daiwa analyst Rick Hsu), 
Ardentec establishes a resilient, differentiated market presence through two structural 
pillars:

• Strategic dual-sourcing anchor and supply chain balancer. As advanced packaging 
and testing capacity is increasingly centralised within leading foundries and mega-
OSATs, tier-1 fabless leaders actively de-risk operations by qualifying secondary 
sources. Ardentec functions as an independent, pure-play testing alternative, providing 
foundry-aligned engineering standards alongside commercial flexibility. This positioning 
allows tier-1 chipmakers to diversify vendor concentration, secure dedicated capacity 
allocations during cyclical tight supply, and mitigate single-point manufacturing 
bottlenecks.

• Technical versatility supported by automotive qualification standards. Ardentec 
maintains a high revenue exposure to automotive and industrial IC testing – sectors that 
demand zero-defect reliability, ultra-wide temperature ranges, and complex mixed-signal 
test protocols. This operational baseline provides significant cross-application versatility. 
During automotive cycle downturns, qualified test cells and probe architectures can be 
reconfigured to support compute, edge AI, and high-frequency communication ICs, 
helping dampen gross margin volatility and stabilising baseline fleet utilisation across 
broader semiconductor cycles.

Longtan: seven-floor mega-fab to scale AI 
Spanning seven vertical cleanroom floors, the company’s Longtan facility began a multi-
year construction timeline starting in 2026, manufacturing semiconductor chips for tier-1 
foundry customers. Management confirmed Floor 1 achieved commercial mass production 
in 3Q26; its +15% MoM revenue inflection in July validated the expansion. At peak 
utilisation, we estimate each fully loaded floor will generate c.TWD150m per month (TWD 
400-500m quarterly) in revenue. With tooling and equipment installations tracking a 
predictable one-floor-per-quarter cadence, each incremental floor can accommodate a 
maximum of 100 high-end automated test equipment (ATE) platforms.

balance sheet risk and secure committed wafer allocation. We believe the fleet is custom-
tailored for leading US hyperscaler ASIC roadmaps. By locking in application-specific test 
setups and eliminating idle downtime associated with frequent load-board changeovers, 
Ardentec is able to achieve industry-leading throughput, uncompromised test cell 
efficiency, and maximised asset turns, per management.

Proprietary accelerator roadmaps across cloud service providers (CSP) – including AWS 
Trainium/Inferentia, Meta MTIA, and Microsoft Maia – feature starkly heterogeneous probe 
card architectures, thermal docking requirements, and test firmware protocols. We believe 
the modular, multi-story footprint at the company’s Longtan facility provides the physical 
isolation necessary to construct dedicated, IP-secure production cleanrooms for multiple 
hyperscaler customers. As custom AI ASIC testing accounts for a rapidly expanding share 
of the company’s revenue mix relative to legacy automotive exposure, we believe this 
structural diversification serves as a strong catalyst for a rerating for Ardentec towards tier-
1 compute OSAT multiples.

Broad-based UTR inflection on peer capacity spillover 
While automotive and consumer segments navigate cyclical normalisation, Ardentec’s non-
AI capacity is staging a powerful recovery, per management. Based on our channel 
checks, its key OSAT peers (eg, KYEC: 2449 TT, not rated) have aggressively reallocated 
leading-edge test fleets toward mega-cap merchant AI GPUs, creating severe capacity 
bottlenecks for mobile application processor (AP), radio frequency (RF), and networking IC 
designers (such as MediaTek – 2454 TT, TWD4,625, Buy [1], covered by Rick Hsu). 
Therefore, Ardentec is capturing these high-volume, displaced wafer sorting (or chip 
probing, CP) and final test (FT) allocations. This order influx swiftly rebounds mature and 
communication line utilisation rates from trough levels (below 65%) back toward the 80%+ 
threshold, turning legacy capacity into a high-cash-generation baseline with zero asset 
drag.

Semiconductor testing carries a high fixed-cost structure heavily weighted toward 
equipment depreciation and engineering overhead. Historically, once aggregate corporate 
UTR crosses the critical 65% fixed-cost absorption threshold, incremental revenue drops 
directly to the bottom line with minimal variable costs (primarily power and consumables).

Next-gen CPO and silicon photonics testing 
Beyond the immediate custom ASIC cycle, Ardentec is securing its next structural growth 
frontier through advanced co-packaged optics (CPO) and silicon photonics (SiPh) wafer-
level testing. As hyperscale AI clusters push copper interconnects to physical transmission 
limits, the industry is transitioning towards optical I/O, which requires testing to shift from 
purely electrical verification to complex electro-optical (E/O) wafer sorting (CP). Unlike 
standard digital logic testing, optical probing demands sub-micron automated fibre 
alignment, precise laser wavelength calibration, optical power loss measurement, and 
specialised thermal-stability testing across photonic integrated circuits (PIC) and electronic 
integrated circuits (EIC) before advanced heterogeneous integration.

Ardentec (3264 TT): 9 September 2026 
Market Analysis 
Structural shift in the IC testing industry 
Reiterating the key message in our Taiwan Testing Interface Initiation: paradigm shift 
accelerated by AI report, we believe the semiconductor testing paradigm is shifting. In the 
multi-die and 2.5D/3D packaging era (eg, TSMC CoWoS, SoIC), wafer testing (chip 
probing, CP) is no longer a commoditised, post-fab defect screening step – it has evolved 
into a mission-critical gating process. We summarise the critical paradigm shift as follows:

• The known good die (KGD) imperative. Heterogeneous integration bundles high-
value compute dies with multiple high bandwidth memory (HBM) stacks. Packaging an 
untested or marginally defective die risks scrapping the entire multi-thousand-dollar 
subsystem. This zero-tolerance threshold elevates CP from sample-based checks to 
exhaustive, full-spec validation across extreme thermal ranges.

• Structural Test-Time Inflation (2-3x). High reticle-limit AI/HPC custom silicon, 
expanding power envelopes (>700W), and thousands of high-speed interconnect 
channels have doubled or tripled test times per wafer. Consequently, automated test 
equipment (ATE) fleet demand is decoupling from raw silicon unit growth, driven instead 
by compounding tester hours.

• Next-Gen CPO Frontier. Rapid silicon tape-out cadences demand high-density vertical 
probe cards (VPC) and micro-cantilever interfaces supporting parallel multi-site testing. 
Beyond electrical testing, the emergence of CPO and SiPh requires active sub-micron 
optical alignment and electro-optical (E/O) sorting, creating multi-year technical barriers 
and pricing premiums for early movers.

Market dynamics: testing outperforms packaging 
According to Gartner (Semiconductor Assembly and Test Services, Worldwide, Sep 2025, 
Semiconductors and Electronics Team), total package and test market size is projected to 
increase from USD47.3bn to USD65.2bn over 2025-29E, representing an 8.4% CAGR 
over the period. Given that third-party market data typically blends packaging and testing 
into total OSAT, we granularly disaggregate historical revenue across major US and Taiwan 
vendors.

While consumer electronics endured a prolonged post-pandemic inventory correction over 
2022-25, the emergence of AI/HPC workloads created a pronounced divergence across 
the backend: historical industry data indicates that the testing sub-segment delivered 
materially higher year-over-year revenue growth than packaging over 2023-25, propelled 
by multi-die KGD mandates and structural test-time inflation.

Advantage of pure-play testing 
We think three reasons distinguish dedicated testing pure-plays from traditional integrated 
OSATs:

• Asset-light model and lean working capital mechanics. Integrated OSATs face 
substantial working capital exposure to expensive ABF substrates, precious metal 
bonding wires, and carrier materials. In contrast, pure-play testing carries virtually zero 
direct material inventory, insulating balance sheets from write-down risks. Furthermore, 
automated test equipment depreciation is concentrated directly within COGS, with 
capital intensity effectively moderated by customer-consigned tester allocations.

Global OSAT: Capex intensity

Source: Bloomberg

• Fleet flexibility and potential spillover orders. Unlike specialised packaging lines that 
require extensive physical retooling, standardised high-end ATE platforms exhibit 
exceptional fleet flexibility. Once test programs and interface hardware are qualified, 
testers can rapidly transition across diverse application domains – from premium mobile 
APs and automotive MCUs to complex custom ASICs – by swapping application-
specific load boards and high-density probe cards. As tier-1 OSATs concentrate their 
tester fleets on merchant AI GPU allocations, mobile AP, 5G RF, and high-speed 
networking chipmakers face severe capacity displacement. We believe pure-play test 
houses could benefit from a dual growth driver: rising demand for high-margin AI/HPC 
programs and incremental mobile and networking orders redirected to underutilised 
legacy tester capacity. This dynamic could decisively shift commercial bargaining 
leverage to pure testers, expanding hourly billing rates and unlocking structural gross 
margin leverage.

• IP neutrality and ecosystem balancing. North American hyperscalers designing 
proprietary custom silicon actively avoid monolithic vendor lock-in and potential IP 
conflicts within integrated supply chains. Pure-play testers provide a strictly neutral

deliver the physical segregation necessary to enforce proprietary security protocols and 
run dedicated engineering cells, allowing pure-play test houses to function as 
indispensable, IP-secure manufacturing partners across the advanced foundry 
ecosystem.

Financial analysis 
Revenue growth in 2026-28E 
We forecast Ardentec’s top-line to enter a multi-year expansion cycle, accelerating from 
TWD14.0bn in 2025 to TWD19.5bn in 2026E (+39.0% YoY), TWD26.8bn in 2027E 
(+37.5% YoY), and reaching TWD34.4bn in 2028E (+28.2% YoY). This represents a 3-year 
revenue CAGR of +34.8% (2025-28E), driven by a powerful confluence of volume 
expansion and structural pricing power across both advanced and mature test fleets.

First, we believe the orderly floor-by-floor commissioning of the 7-story Longtan facility 
delivers a predictable volume baseline, with each fully loaded cleanroom floor contributing 
c.TWD150m per month (c.TWD450m+ quarterly) in incremental run-rate revenue. Beyond 
pure wafer throughput, we expect revenue quality to see a significant mix upgrade, as 
advanced wafer sorting for hyperscaler AI ASICs and complex HPC processors command 
an hourly billing rate of nearly double (95-100% premium) that of traditional automotive and 
industrial IC testing, fundamentally elevating revenue density per tester hour.

Furthermore, as Ardentec prioritises engineering bandwidth, cleanroom footprint, and 
tester allocations toward high-margin advanced CP, legacy and mature-node test capacity 
faces internal supply tightness. This self-induced capacity crowding shifts pricing leverage 
back to Ardentec, allowing the company to enact upward price adjustments across lagging-
node test services and accelerating a broad-based, corporate-wide blended ASP 
expansion.

Ardentec (3264 TT): 9 September 2026 
Gross margin inflection driven by UTR surge  
We believe capacity utilisation rate serves as the single most critical determinant of 
Ardentec’s gross margin trajectory. Over the coming quarters, we project a structural 
rebound in corporate-wide utilisation driven by 2 powerful operational forces.

First, the rapid ramp of the Longtan advanced testing fab—operating at an optimal 85-90% 
UTR ceiling—will represent a rapidly expanding proportion of total capacity, immediately 
enriching the blended margin profile. Second, incoming spillover orders from tier-1 
communications and mobile AP clients are directly refilling legacy and mature testing lines. 
Because these mature tools are almost or already fully depreciated, incremental spillover 
revenue incurs virtually zero depreciation burden, generating exceptionally high operating 
leverage and outsized gross profit contribution, in our view.

Therefore, we estimate that this dual dynamic of high-end capacity scaling and fully 
amortised tool revitalisation will propel gross margins from 36.3% in 2025 to 42.3% in 
2026E, 44.5% in 2027E and reach 45.0% in 2028E.

Ardentec: gross margin

Source: Company, Daiwa forecasts

Earnings led by gross margin amid subdued operating 
leverage 
We believe that Ardentec’s earnings inflection is predominantly a story of gross margin and 
operating leverage rather than structural opex dilution. The high-fixed-cost nature of the 
wafer testing business—governed by multi-billion-dollar ATE depreciation and cleanroom 
overhead—is heavily concentrated within Cost of Goods Sold (COGS), per management. 
Consequently, the powerful fixed-cost absorption resulting from Longtan’s ramp and peer 
spillover orders directly fuels gross margin expansion, while operating leverage at the 
SG&A/opex level remains comparatively subdued.

Because high-end ASIC and electro-optical testing require tailored test program 
development, application-specific load board debugging and dedicated test engineering 
headcount for each CSP roadmap, we believe R&D and engineering talent function as 
quasi-variable expenses that scale in tandem with incremental floor additions. As a result, 
we project Ardentec’s opex-to-sales ratio to remain relatively stable in the 9-10% corridor 
over 2026-28E.

Balance sheet and cash flow 
While management has not provided official guidance due to dynamic cleanroom 
construction milestones and equipment lead-time variations, we model an orderly 
deployment cadence of one cleanroom floor per quarter at the Longtan mega-facility. 
Under this baseline, we forecast total capital expenditures (capex) to step up aggressively 
to TWD14.5bn in 2026E and TWD18.5bn in 2027E to support facility buildout and early tool 
installations.

To fund this expansion phase, Ardentec intends to prioritise low-cost bank borrowings over 
issuing overseas or domestic convertible bonds (ECB/CB), deliberately avoiding equity 
dilution to maximise EPS accretion during the upcoming earnings supercycle. 
Concurrently, management plans to optimise capital allocation by pragmatically calibrating 
its historical cash dividend pay-out ratio. We estimate the pay-out ratio will moderate 
slightly from 70% to 50% to retain internal cash for high-return AI capacity. Meanwhile, we 
believe the company actively negotiates customer-consigned tester models for subsequent 
cleanroom floors. This is a framework that insulates the cost structure from severe, front-
loaded depreciation burdens and channels capital directly into superior return on equity 
(ROE) growth.

Benefitting from disciplined financing, high asset turnover on advanced wafer sort lines and 
gross margin expansion, we project Ardentec's ROE to stage a structural recovery from the 
cyclical trough experienced during the 2023 automotive downturn, steadily increasing from 
15.1% to 32.1% over 2025-2027E.

Ardentec (3264 TT): 9 September 2026 
Valuation and recommendation 
Initiating coverage with a Buy (1) rating 
Given the current structural mix transition from cyclical automotives towards high-margin 
AI/HPC custom ASICs, amplified gross margin and operating leverage from the Longtan 
ramp, and ROE inflection likely to surpass its 2022 historical peak of c.23% over 2026-
28E, we initiate coverage of Ardentec with a Buy (1) rating and 12-month TP of TWD340, 
based on a target PER of 25.0x applied to our 4-quarter-forward EPS. Our target multiple 
of 25.0x is supported by a 48% EPS CAGR over 2025-28E (PEG of c.0.52x).

Historically, Ardentec traded within a compressed band of 10-25x forward PER due to its 
cyclical automotive and industrial exposure. While the stock has begun to re-rate alongside 
recent EPS upgrades, we view this multiple expansion as far from complete. In addition, 
we believe Ardentec still trades at a noticeable discount (14.9x PER on 2027E EPS) vs. 
leading AI testing peers (KYEC trading at 18.7x PER, while ASE at 26.4x), leaving ample 
room for multiple expansion as its pure-play test economics and earnings growth outpace 
the broader backend sector.

### Page 17

17

Ardentec (3264 TT): 9 September 2026 
Risks to our call 
1. Delays in capacity expansion (primary risk). Delays in Longtan cleanroom and 
equipment installations, or customer qualifications could disrupt the floor-by-floor ramp, 
deferring high-margin hyperscaler ASIC revenue and diluting near-term gross margin 
leverage.  
2. Pricing pressure as a second-source supplier (secondary risk). Serving as a 
second-source supplier forces Ardentec to accept lower hourly rates than primary 
competitors. Furthermore, non-AI clients demand annual price cuts, limiting gross 
margin recovery unless capacity utilisation remains exceptionally high.  
3. Slow automotive and industrial recovery (secondary risk). Prolonged inventory 
adjustments in automotive and industrial markets continue to drag down IDM revenue 
share. Clients are placing short-term urgent orders rather than rebuilding long-term 
inventory, creating demand uncertainty for mature testing capacity.

### Page 18

18

Ardentec (3264 TT): 9 September 2026 
Appendix 
Company introduction 
Founded in 1999 and headquartered in Hsinchu, Ardentec is an independent 
semiconductor testing service provider. The company specialises in wafer testing (wafer 
sort) and final testing for memory, logic and mixed-signal integrated circuits. Its diverse 
portfolio addresses several sectors, including automotive electronics, 5G communications, 
storage and advanced AI/HPC ASICs.

Through strategic investments in Giga Solution and Raytek, Ardentec has also successfully 
expanded into RF testing and bumping capabilities. Its global customer base spans world-
class foundries, IDMs and fabless designers, such as MediaTek, Marvell, Texas 
Instruments, Infineon and NXP.

Ardentec’s core competitive advantage lies in its early leadership in automotive 
semiconductor testing, underpinned by ISO 26262 certification and stringent reliability 
standards that support strong customer stickiness and order stability. As an independent, 
pure-play testing provider, Ardentec serves as a neutral strategic partner to leading fabless 
companies, offering foundry-grade engineering capabilities, dedicated capacity and greater 
commercial flexibility.

Its role extends beyond that of a conventional second-source supplier: by providing an 
independent alternative to increasingly consolidated foundry and OSAT ecosystems, 
Ardentec helps customers diversify supply-chain risk, secure critical testing capacity and 
avoid potential bottlenecks. Backed by its state-of-the-art Longtan facility dedicated to 
advanced AI ASIC testing and a global manufacturing footprint across Taiwan, Nanjing, 
South Korea and Singapore, Ardentec is well positioned as an indispensable testing 
partner for tier-1 and emerging semiconductor customers.

Shareholder structure 
Ardentec’s shareholder base is well diversified, with no single controlling shareholder. 
Dresdner Bank AG is the largest holder with an 8.36% stake, followed by Macronix 
International (7.33%) and Vanguard Group (4.66%). The register also includes long-term 
institutional investors such as BlackRock, Dimensional Fund Advisors, BNP Paribas and 
Taiwan’s pension-related funds, providing a stable ownership structure and supporting 
governance transparency.

Capital management 2

management approach.

transaction 1

directors are required to disclose conflicts and abstain from related decisions.

### Page 2

iPhone 18 Pro and Pro Max: (1) Cooling: the new iPhone is embedded with a more 
powerful SoC (A20 Pro) and Apple Intelligence, making thermal management essential 
to the device. The next generation of Vapor Chamber is used in the new iPhones, which 
we see as positive for the cooling suppliers. Read more: AVC, AAC; (2) Camera: variable 
aperture is adopted in rear cameras, in which lens aperture changes based on the focal 
length, allowing easier user control of DoF (depth of field), taking pictures in low lighting, 
or having a more natural broken gradient background when in portrait mode. Read 
more: Largan. Selling price is higher by US$100 (or high single-digit %) compared to 
iPhone 17 Pro series, with the same delivery time.  
Apple Watch and Airpods: Airpods 5 feature active noise cancellation, and are 
designed to allow more natural interaction (e.g. transparency mode, better conversation 
awareness). The wireless charging case models are US$30 cheaper compared to Airpods 
4. Apple Watch 12 and Ultra 4 are priced the same as the previous generation with 
better specifications: health sensing system with the most accurate heart rate sensing in 
a wearable, Siri recap, audio intelligence, etc.

3443 創意(GUC)

報告日期： 2026/09/09 研 究 員：羅  金 鳳 (Sunny Lo)  
壹、投 資 建 議

一. CPU 續強外， ADAS 開始貢獻， 2H26 營運正向  
創意 2Q26 因 NRE 進入 Tape-out 及 Turkey 明顯出貨，使雲端相
關產品 (包括 Compute 、Connectivity) 佔營收比重達 80%，消費性
產品、記憶體、虛擬貨幣合計佔約 20% ，帶動 2Q26 整體營收達
138.97 億元， QoQ+21.39% ，YoY+127.64% ，毛利率因 Turkey
大幅上升而由 1Q26 的 27.2% 下滑至 21.55% ，稅後 EPS 11.61
元，合計 1H26 稅後 EPS 23.89 元，符合預期。

2H26 北美客戶 CPU 持續放量出貨，帶動 7、8 月平均單月營收約
58 億元，優於預期，使雲端產品營收比重維持在 80% 左右，加上
來自於北美、大陸、台灣的車用產品貢獻上升，取代大部份虛擬
貨幣的下滑，使 2H26 營收獲利加速， 2026 年呈現逐季成長的營
運走勢 ， 且在費用維持穩定(僅研發費用增加 )下 ， 我們預估創3Q26
營收 169.57 億元， QoQ+22.02% ，YoY+96.87% ，將優於市場預
期的 QoQ+10%， 單季稅後EPS 14.37 元，預 估4Q26 營收 222.36
億元， QoQ+31.13% ，YoY+79.33% ，稅後 EPS 19.42 元(因客戶
拉貨需求強勁，預估其中一個月的單月營收將創新高 )。

二. CPU 第二代產品將於 4Q26 ～1Q27 Tape-out 
創意除了車用產品將開始開花結果 ， 中系客戶於2H26 開始出貨，
美系客戶亦將於 2H27 開始出貨貢獻營收，且美系客戶的營收貢
獻將遠大於中系客戶 ， 預估將使創意車用營收比重由2026 年的個
位數百分比提升至 2027 年的雙位數百分比 ， 營收呈現超過倍數成
長，為 2027 年營運成長兩大動能之一；創意 CPU 客戶的第一代
產品已大量生產，第二代產品亦將於 4Q26 年底到 1Q27 年進行
Tape-out ，預估 2027 年底開始量產。在產能部份，雖然晶圓代工
廠產能緊俏，但創意憑藉與晶圓代工龍頭深厚的關係，預估 2027
年產能的取得將優於 2026 年，推動創意營收獲利顯著成長。

三. 預估 2026/2027 年稅後 EPS 57.68/131.35 元 
2026 年虛擬貨幣仍持續貢獻營收，但在雲端相關的產品放量生產
帶動下，營收獲利將明顯跳升，我們預估創意 2026 年整體營收達
645.38 億元 ，YoY+89.03% ， 毛利率22.2%，稅 後 淨 利77.3 億元，
YoY+105.06% ，稅後 EPS 57.68 元。在 CPU 及車用產品大幅貢
獻的帶動下，我們預估創意 2027 年整體營收 1,363.47 億元，

四. 投資建議買進，目標價 7881 元 
2026 年在在 CSP 及 AI 量產貢獻大幅成長的帶動下，營收及獲利
將呈現明顯跳升的表現，且因 CSP 客戶的下一代晶片訂單也已確
定取得，及車用晶片貢獻放量之下， 2027 年營收獲利將進一步明
顯成長，因此，投資建議買進，目標價 7881 元(60X2027 年稅後
EPS) 。

貳、公 司 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介  
創意 (3443) 成立於 1998 年 ， 目前台積電持有35% 的股份 。 創意主
要以提供客戶特殊應用積體電路 (ASIC) 設計服務，包括 Spec-in
和系統單晶片整合 (SoC Integration) 、實體設計 (Physical 
Design) 、先進封裝技術 (Advanced Packaging Technology) 、量產
服務、 HBM 及 die-to-die 互連 IP，可應用於人工智慧 (AI) 、高速
運算 (HPC) 、5G / 網路、固態硬碟 (SSD) 和工業領域等市場。在先
進製程面臨摩爾定律極限後，半導體技術轉向利用先進封裝來增
加晶片的效能，使得先進封裝的發展重要性大幅提升，創意電子
亦專注於先進封裝技術的研發，包括 CoWoS，InFO 和 3D SoIC ，
滿足客戶各種需求。

二.營運概況及展望  
1、ASIC 產業重要性日益提升 
10/2022 年微軟推出 ChatGPT ，為科技產業帶來新的亮點，生成
式 AI 的新體驗，驚豔全球使用者，開啟各種終端產品導入 AI 的
功能，以提升產品效能及消費者使用滿意度，對廠商的正面幫助
是可因此提升產品 /製造的內含價值，提升公司獲利能力。由於 AI
的發展需要依靠規格較高、算力較強的伺服器來實現卓越的效
能，帶動 AI Server 建置的風潮。 AI Server 效能的展現端賴 GPU
強大的平行運算效能加上 AI 加速器的應用來強化 AI 功能的表
現，而 AI Server 算力模型的建置風潮，帶動高效能的 GPU 晶片
呈現供不應求的情況，在奇貨可居的情形下，使得原本價格就相
當高昂的晶片 GPU 晶片 ， 價格更是水漲船高 ， 促使建置AI Server
的國際大廠 Amazon 、Google 、Microsoft 、Meta 等，以及新創半
導體 IC 設計公司，紛紛展開研發自有晶片的行動，期以自研晶片
來取代高昂的 GPU 晶片，來降低成本，且保有設計自主性及產品
異化，使得特殊應用積體電路 (ASIC) 產業的重要性日益提升，除

了帶動 IP( 矽智財 )產業地位水漲船高外 ， 委託設計服務(NRE) 及晶
片量產的業務亦受到產業重視。 2030 年 AI 將加速帶動半導體產
值由 2021 年的 5,000 萬美元，大幅成長至 1 兆美元，其中全球
IP( 智慧財產權 )市場將達到 18 億美元， 2024 年至 2030 年 CAGR

由於創意擁有 IP、良好的設計服務及投片量產經驗，以及與晶圓
代工廠長期良好的配合，雖然目前公司規模相對仍小，但憑藉優
異的設計能力，可同時提供客戶前後段的設計服務，可望吸引國
際大廠合作，在 AI 蓬勃發展的半導體商機中，無論是 NRE 或是
CoWoS 的商機將大幅成長 ， 創意可望獲得相關技術產品的營收貢
獻。

公司簡介 
鈞興為高精度中小模數齒輪製造商 ， 產品為
各式齒輪 、 齒輪箱及五金件之設計 、 製造及
銷售 ， 產品主要應用於手持式電動工具 、 園
林工具 、 工業用縫紉機及自動化設備等 ， 主
要銷售市場為美國、歐洲及中國大陸等地
區。 產品應用領域以電動工具佔比最高
75%， 園林工具領域佔比11.5%， 公司齒輪
精密度以 9 級以上的中高精密度齒輪為主。

主要客戶：TTI、松下、牧田、喜利得 
主要競爭對手：

林其美 iris.lin@sinopac.com 
本次報告：法說會

鈞興-KY (4571 TT) 
成長潛力醞釀中

永豐觀點 
電動工具與園林工具溫和成長 ，人型機器人關節產品與靈巧手有機會成為未來新成
長動能。 
 投資評價與建議 
維持買進：理由包括：(1) 電動工具與園林工具市場每年成長 4%-6%，今年下半
年電動工具有新品鋪貨，全年營收個位數成長，預估2027 年亦維持溫和成長，(2) 
智能傳動今年營收將成長 21%，其中工業機器人齒輪接單熱絡，預料 2027 年成
長動能持續 ，(3)人型機器人關節產品與靈巧手零件送樣中 ， 若通過將於2027 年量
產，將是未來成長的另一波推手。在評價方面，品牌廠 TTI、Stanley Blac 本益比
已來到 16-20 倍，公司營運穩健成長，人型機器人關節產品與靈巧手有機會成為
新成長動能，維持買進建議，目標價 220 元（20 X 2027EPS）。 
 近八季營收及 YoY 趨勢圖

### Page 2

2

公司簡介：鈞興為高精度中小模數齒輪製造商，產品為各式齒輪、齒輪箱及五金件
之設計、製造及銷售，產品主要應用於手持式電動工具、園林工具、工業用縫紉機
及自動化設備等，主要銷售市場為美國、歐洲及中國大陸等地區。客戶幾乎皆為該
行業的國際領導品牌，客戶包括 TTI、松下、牧田、喜利得等等。公司從 OEM 開
始製作銷售平車齒輪，以台資廠商技術，給予比日本廠商更有競爭力的價格，再從
縫紉機跨足其他領域深耕技術優勢，延伸到電動工具、園林工具，營運模式也從
OEM 轉為 ODM。產品應用領域以電動工具佔比最高 75%，園林工具領域佔比
11.5%，公司齒輪精密度以 9 級以上的中高精密度齒輪為主。 
鈞興 2Q26 EPS 為 1.72 元，業外因人民幣升值而有匯兌損失：鈞興-KY 2Q26 合
併營收為 7.28 億元（+8.15%YoY） ，毛利率34.24%，較去年同期提升 3.92 個百
分點，主要原因是(1) 產品結構優化，毛利率相對較低的齒輪箱與粉末冶金佔比下
降，其他產品如智能傳動等比重上升，(2) 越南工廠生產銷售的規模經濟發酵，毛
利率提升，上述兩因素抵銷人民幣升值的不利因素。營業費用較高，乃因庫存股轉
讓給員工費用達 3185 萬元，高於去年同期的 1280 萬元。營業利 1.11 億元，業
外有人民幣升值帶來的匯兌損失 1581 萬元，稅後淨利 0.91 億元（-0.68%YoY），
EPS 為 1.72 元。 
下半年電動工具新品出貨，營收將個位數成長；電動工具約有 30%-40%應用在房
地產相關領域，受美國通膨、升息預期影響，客戶下單並未太積極 （不過終端庫存
也不多） ，然客戶下半年將推出新品，初期鋪貨量會較多，綜合上述兩因素，下半
年電動工具預計個位數成長，園林工具則持平至小幅衰退。展望 2027 年，預期電
動工具維持個位數成長，園林工具在正常庫存回補下，有機會慢慢好轉。 
智能傳動今年營收將成長 21%，其中佔比最大的工業機器人齒輪成長勢頭將延續
至 2027 年：智能傳動 1H26 佔比 10.44%，產品包括工業機器人齒輪、減速工具
齒輪、諧波減速器與自行車中置電機 ，預計智能傳動全年營收將成長 21%YoY： 
(1) 工業機器人齒輪 1H26 營收 1.26 億元，佔營收比重 8.33%，成長 46%YoY，
兩家大客戶 （日本與中國） 是成長動能所在。2025 年該產品營收 1.78 億元，目前
接單已有 2.6 億元，不過部分是明年上半年出貨，今年預計出貨額可達 2.3 億元。
由於自動化行業需求暢旺，2027 年工業機器人齒輪成長動能將持續。另外，公司
將與該日本客戶合作，打入其他日本客戶齒輪供應鏈中。(2) 減速工具齒輪客戶為
德系自動化設備廠商，2022 年俄烏戰爭後自動化設備開始衰退，去年下半年減幅
縮小，今年出貨好轉，預計可成長 10%。(3) 諧波減速器 1H26 營收 323 萬元，
目前已接單 2000 萬元，預計今年營收可達 1300 萬元（去年 900-1000 萬元） 。
(4)自行車中置電機訂單狀況未達預期，目前訂單有將外銷日本的小徑車，但仍在
等待取得日本電子認證，預計 2027 年將有數百台的出貨量。另外公司將開發電子
無段變速系統，產品將於明年正式亮相。 
人型機器人關節產品與靈巧手零件送樣中，若通過將於2027 年量產：人形機器人
目前接洽的進度包括(1)應用在醫療的台灣廠商，產品為關節模組零件， 客戶進行
測試中，(2)深圳一家系統整合商關節模組採用公司漸開線少齒差產品，可克服使
用諧波減速機所產生的散熱問題，目前送樣給客戶測試，需耗時 2-3 個月，若通
過，依客戶規格再修正後有機會於 2027 年量產，(3) 微型齒輪應用在靈巧手將送
樣給美系客戶，若通過可於 2027 年量產，2028 年需購地擴充產能。 
產能狀況 —越南二期 4Q26 將陸續進設備： 在產能方面，珠海與越南廠產能約
70%：30%，珠海工廠電動與園林工具產能利用率八成，機器人相關產能利用率九
成至九成五；越南廠下半年訂單狀況依舊好，但今年招工比較困難，產能利用率超

營運現況與分析

### Page 3

3

過 100%；越南二期九月底完工、第四季準備進設備，估計四至五年才會滿載。 
財務預估 ：預估 2026 年營收可達 31.65 億元(+3.28%YoY)，營業利益 5.72 億元，
稅後淨利 5.09 億元(+2.21%YoY)，EPS 為 9.6 元。預估 2027 年營收可達 35 億元
(+10.56%YoY)，營業利益 6.72 億元，稅後淨利 5.95 億元(+16.91%YoY)，EPS 為
11.22 元。

1 
4770 上品(ASC)

報告日期： 2026/09/07           研究員：王蕎程 (Joe Wang) 
壹、投 資 建 議

一.漲價轉嫁與高毛利訂單出貨，2Q26 稅後 EPS 3.11 元 
上品主要產品為氟素樹脂「鐵氟龍」半成品材料生產、內襯設備
加工及工程服務，半導體應用佔比近 9 成。2Q26 營收 13.0 億元，
QoQ 23.0%，YoY 0%；營業毛利 4.7 億元，QoQ 25.6%，YoY -5.2%，
毛利率 35.6%較去年同期下降 1.74 個百分點，主係部分出貨來自
去年景氣相對疲弱時接下的訂單，議價空間受限、毛利率偏低，
隨著下半年漲價轉嫁與高毛利訂單陸續出貨，預計毛利率將回
升；營業利益 3.0 億元，QoQ 42.3%，YoY -15.1%，營益率 23.0%；
稅後純益 2.5 億元，QoQ 23.4%，YoY 13.0%，純益率 19.1%，稅後
EPS 3.11 元。

7 月營收 5.3 億元，MoM 12.0%，YoY 47.7%；1-7 月累計營收 28.9
億元，YoY 9.6%。

二. 半導體建廠帶動的化學品供應 ， 訂單能見度至 28 年 
產品組合方面，內襯裝置佔 68.3%；其他材料與內襯管路分別佔
16.7% 與 9.6%；工程收入佔 5.4%。內襯裝置部分，隨著先進製程
對 particle(顆粒污染)、metal contamination(金屬污染)的要求越來越
嚴格，上品的材料升級能力可以配合 2 奈米以下製程需求，從原
料端到終端裝置完整供應。

上品中國市場營收佔比約 44.8% ，中國市場毛利率 33% ，略低於台
灣的 36%，對整體獲利結構的影響較高。台灣市場佔比約 49.3%，
受半導體產業發展持續帶動，持續有大量專案進行中；美國市場
主要客戶二廠槽車專案，從每月 80 台提升到 140 台，目前洽談訂
單已有 400 餘輛，其中 100 台已下訂並準備運往美國，預估今年
ISO tank 出貨量將較去年同期成長 80%至 90%，需求強勁。

三. 預估 2026 / 2027 稅後 EPS 12.5 / 14.5 元 
預估 2026 年上品逐步自 2025 年底逐月回升，2026 年第二季受集
中出貨帶動，7 月營收已突破 5 億元，預計下半年表現優於上半
年，全年營收預估 51.0 億元，YoY 8.0% ；營業毛利 18.2 億元，YoY 
16.9%，毛利率 35.7%；營業利益 11.8 億元，稅後純益 10.0 億元，
稅後 EPS 12.5 元。

考量台灣及美國半導體廠建廠帶動長期需求，預估 2027 年全年營
收 58.7 億元，YoY 15.0%，營業毛利 21.1 億元，稅後純益 11.6 億
元，稅後 EPS 14.5 元。

四. 投資建議為「買進」，目標價 275 元 
綜合考量 (1)台灣、美國半導體建廠需求強勁 (2)中國同業競爭激
烈使毛利率受到壓抑 (3)全球半導體市場維持穩定成長步調 (4)
上品技術領先，於台灣市場競爭對手少具寡佔地位，並觀察上品
歷史本益比區間 ， 目前本益比 15.6 倍評價合理 ， 故投資建議為 「買
進」，目標價 275 元（19 x 2027 EPS）。

貳、公 司 概 況

一. 公司簡介 
上品綜合工業股份有限公司（簡稱：上品；代號：4770）成立於
1981 年，總部位於台灣彰化，為全球少數具備從氟素樹脂原料加
工、半成品製造到內襯設備整合能力的一條龍供應商。公司核心
產品涵蓋氟素內襯設備、內襯管件及應用材料，廣泛應用於半導
體及電子級化學品產業的高純化學品儲存、輸送與製程系統，並
服務面板、石化等高腐蝕性化學環境應用領域。上品透過垂直整
合掌握關鍵原料與製程，能提供完整的客製化解決方案，確保產
品在耐腐蝕性、潔淨度及可靠性上的領先水準。公司於台灣彰濱
設有生產基地與研發中心，在中國浙江嘉興及美國亞利桑那均設
有百分之百持股子公司，結合當地業務與工程服務團隊，支援全
球客戶的建廠與設備需求。

截至 2026 年，半導體與電子化學品產業為上品營收主力，合計貢
獻超過八成，客戶涵蓋晶圓製造與先進化學品大廠。隨全球先進
製程擴產與電子級化學品本地化政策推進，公司積極參與台灣、
美國與中國多個新建廠案，並在高階氟素內襯產品市場形成與少
數日系廠商的寡佔格局。
